        "from googleapiclient.discovery import build\n",
        "from IPython.display import display, HTML, clear_output\n",
        "import ipywidgets as widgets\n",
        "import yt_dlp, requests\n",
        "\n",
        "API_KEY = \"AIzaSyBsxJpm4Ifbpk1qUzDhLC9757dN8ZfO-7A\"  # Get from console.cloud.google.com\n",
        "youtube = build(\"youtube\", \"v3\", developerKey=API_KEY)\n",
        "\n",
        "def fmt_num(n):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        n = int(n)\n",
        "        if n >= 1_000_000: return f\"{n/1_000_000:.1f}M\"\n",
        "        if n >= 1_000:     return f\"{n/1_000:.1f}K\"\n",
        "        return str(n)\n",
        "    except: return \"N/A\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def fmt_duration(seconds):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        s = int(seconds)\n",
        "        h, m, sec = s//3600, (s%3600)//60, s%60\n",
        "        return f\"{h}:{m:02d}:{sec:02d}\" if h else f\"{m}:{sec:02d}\"\n",
        "    except: return \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"✅ Setup done!\")"
      ],

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "def search_videos(query, max_results=12):\n",
        "    res = youtube.search().list(\n",
        "        part=\"snippet\", q=query, type=\"video\",\n",
        "        maxResults=max_results, order=\"relevance\"\n",
        "    ).execute()\n",
        "\n",
        "    ids = [i[\"id\"][\"videoId\"] for i in res.get(\"items\", [])]\n",
        "    details = youtube.videos().list(\n",
        "        part=\"snippet,statistics,contentDetails\",\n",
        "        id=\",\".join(ids)\n",
        "    ).execute()\n",
        "\n",
        "    videos = []\n",
        "    for item in details.get(\"items\", []):\n",
        "        s = item[\"snippet\"]\n",
        "        st = item.get(\"statistics\", {})\n",
        "        import isodate\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            dur = isodate.parse_duration(item[\"contentDetails\"][\"duration\"])\n",
        "            dur_str = fmt_duration(int(dur.total_seconds()))\n",
        "        except: dur_str = \"\"\n",
        "        videos.append({\n",
        "            \"id\":          item[\"id\"],\n",
        "            \"title\":       s[\"title\"],\n",
        "            \"channel\":     s[\"channelTitle\"],\n",
        "            \"thumbnail\":   s[\"thumbnails\"][\"medium\"][\"url\"],\n",
        "            \"views\":       st.get(\"viewCount\", 0),\n",
        "            \"likes\":       st.get(\"likeCount\", 0),\n",
        "            \"duration\":    dur_str,\n",
        "            \"published\":   s[\"publishedAt\"][:10],\n",
        "            \"description\": s.get(\"description\",\"\")[:400],\n",
        "        })\n",
        "    return videos\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_stream_url(video_id):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Use yt-dlp to get direct CDN stream URL — no youtube.com in browser.\"\"\"\n",
        "    ydl_opts = {\n",
        "        \"format\": \"best[ext=mp4][height<=480]/best[ext=mp4]/best\",\n",
        "        \"quiet\": True,\n",
        "        \"no_warnings\": True,\n",
        "    }\n",
        "    with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:\n",
        "        info = ydl.extract_info(\n",
        "            f\"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={video_id}\",\n",
        "            download=False\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        return info[\"url\"], info.get(\"title\",\"\"), int(info.get(\"duration\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_trending(max_results=12):\n",
        "    res = youtube.videos().list(\n",
        "        part=\"snippet,statistics,contentDetails\",\n",
        "        chart=\"mostPopular\", regionCode=\"IN\",\n",
        "        maxResults=max_results\n",
        "    ).execute()\n",
        "    videos = []\n",
        "    for item in res.get(\"items\", []):\n",
        "        s = item[\"snippet\"]\n",
        "        st = item.get(\"statistics\", {})\n",
        "        import isodate\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            dur = isodate.parse_duration(item[\"contentDetails\"][\"duration\"])\n",
        "            dur_str = fmt_duration(int(dur.total_seconds()))\n",
        "        except: dur_str = \"\"\n",
        "        videos.append({\n",
        "            \"id\":          item[\"id\"],\n",
        "            \"title\":       s[\"title\"],\n",
        "            \"channel\":     s[\"channelTitle\"],\n",
        "            \"thumbnail\":   s[\"thumbnails\"][\"medium\"][\"url\"],\n",
        "            \"views\":       st.get(\"viewCount\", 0),\n",
        "            \"likes\":       st.get(\"likeCount\", 0),\n",
        "            \"duration\":    dur_str,\n",
        "            \"published\":   s[\"publishedAt\"][:10],\n",
        "            \"description\": s.get(\"description\",\"\")[:400],\n",
        "        })\n",
        "    return videos\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"✅ Functions ready!\")"

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "def get_stream_urls(video_id):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Fetch multiple quality stream URLs in one yt-dlp call.\"\"\"\n",
        "    ydl_opts = {\n",
        "        \"quiet\": True,\n",
        "        \"no_warnings\": True,\n",
        "        \"format\": \"bestvideo[ext=mp4]+bestaudio[ext=m4a]/best[ext=mp4]/best\",\n",
        "    }\n",
        "    quality_map = {}\n",
        "    with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL({\"quiet\": True, \"no_warnings\": True}) as ydl:\n",
        "        info = ydl.extract_info(\n",
        "            f\"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={video_id}\", download=False\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        formats = info.get(\"formats\", [])\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Pick best URL per height bucket\n",
        "        seen = set()\n",
        "        for f in reversed(formats):\n",
        "            h = f.get(\"height\")\n",
        "            ext = f.get(\"ext\", \"\")\n",
        "            url = f.get(\"url\", \"\")\n",
        "            if not h or not url or ext not in (\"mp4\", \"webm\", \"m4a\", \"\"):\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "            label = f\"{h}p\"\n",
        "            if label not in seen and h in (144, 240, 360, 480, 720, 1080):\n",
        "                quality_map[label] = url\n",
        "                seen.add(label)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Fallback: grab the single best\n",
        "        if not quality_map:\n",
        "            with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL({\n",
        "                \"quiet\": True, \"no_warnings\": True,\n",
        "                \"format\": \"best[ext=mp4]/best\"\n",
        "            }) as ydl2:\n",
        "                info2 = ydl2.extract_info(\n",
        "                    f\"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={video_id}\", download=False\n",
        "                )\n",
        "                quality_map[\"Auto\"] = info2[\"url\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Sort highest to lowest\n",
        "        order = [\"1080p\",\"720p\",\"480p\",\"360p\",\"240p\",\"144p\",\"Auto\"]\n",
        "        sorted_map = {k: quality_map[k] for k in order if k in quality_map}\n",
        "        return sorted_map, info.get(\"title\",\"\"), int(info.get(\"duration\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"✅ Multi-quality fetcher ready!\")"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import os\n",
        "import base64\n",
        "import isodate\n",
        "import yt_dlp\n",
        "\n",
        "# Keep deno in PATH\n",
        "os.environ[\"PATH\"] = os.environ[\"PATH\"] + \":/root/.deno/bin\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# Base yt-dlp options used everywhere\n",
        "YDL_BASE_OPTS = {\n",
        "    \"quiet\":       True,\n",
        "    \"no_warnings\": True,\n",
        "    \"extractor_args\": {\n",
        "        \"youtube\": {\n",
        "            \"player_client\": [\"web\", \"web_safari\"],\n",
        "        }\n",
        "    },\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Formatters ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def fmt_num(n):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        n = int(n)\n",
        "        if n >= 1_000_000: return f\"{n/1_000_000:.1f}M\"\n",
        "        if n >= 1_000:     return f\"{n/1_000:.1f}K\"\n",
        "        return str(n)\n",
        "    except: return \"N/A\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def fmt_duration(seconds):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        s = int(seconds)\n",
        "        h, m, sec = s//3600, (s%3600)//60, s%60\n",
        "        return f\"{h}:{m:02d}:{sec:02d}\" if h else f\"{m}:{sec:02d}\"\n",
        "    except: return \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── YouTube API search ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def search_videos(query, max_results=12):\n",
        "    res = youtube.search().list(\n",
        "        part=\"snippet\", q=query, type=\"video\",\n",
        "        maxResults=max_results, order=\"relevance\"\n",
        "    ).execute()\n",
        "    ids = [i[\"id\"][\"videoId\"] for i in res.get(\"items\", [])]\n",
        "    details = youtube.videos().list(\n",
        "        part=\"snippet,statistics,contentDetails\",\n",
        "        id=\",\".join(ids)\n",
        "    ).execute()\n",
        "    videos = []\n",
        "    for item in details.get(\"items\", []):\n",
        "        s  = item[\"snippet\"]\n",
        "        st = item.get(\"statistics\", {})\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            dur = isodate.parse_duration(item[\"contentDetails\"][\"duration\"])\n",
        "            dur_str = fmt_duration(int(dur.total_seconds()))\n",
        "        except:\n",
        "            dur_str = \"\"\n",
        "        videos.append({\n",
        "            \"id\":          item[\"id\"],\n",
        "            \"title\":       s[\"title\"],\n",
        "            \"channel\":     s[\"channelTitle\"],\n",
        "            \"thumbnail\":   s[\"thumbnails\"][\"medium\"][\"url\"],\n",
        "            \"views\":       st.get(\"viewCount\", 0),\n",
        "            \"likes\":       st.get(\"likeCount\", 0),\n",
        "            \"duration\":    dur_str,\n",
        "            \"published\":   s[\"publishedAt\"][:10],\n",
        "            \"description\": s.get(\"description\", \"\")[:400],\n",
        "        })\n",
        "    return videos\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── YouTube API trending ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def get_trending(max_results=12):\n",
        "    res = youtube.videos().list(\n",
        "        part=\"snippet,statistics,contentDetails\",\n",
        "        chart=\"mostPopular\", regionCode=\"IN\",\n",
        "        maxResults=max_results\n",
        "    ).execute()\n",
        "    videos = []\n",
        "    for item in res.get(\"items\", []):\n",
        "        s  = item[\"snippet\"]\n",
        "        st = item.get(\"statistics\", {})\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            dur = isodate.parse_duration(item[\"contentDetails\"][\"duration\"])\n",
        "            dur_str = fmt_duration(int(dur.total_seconds()))\n",
        "        except:\n",
        "            dur_str = \"\"\n",
        "        videos.append({\n",
        "            \"id\":          item[\"id\"],\n",
        "            \"title\":       s[\"title\"],\n",
        "            \"channel\":     s[\"channelTitle\"],\n",
        "            \"thumbnail\":   s[\"thumbnails\"][\"medium\"][\"url\"],\n",
        "            \"views\":       st.get(\"viewCount\", 0),\n",
        "            \"likes\":       st.get(\"likeCount\", 0),\n",
        "            \"duration\":    dur_str,\n",
        "            \"published\":   s[\"publishedAt\"][:10],\n",
        "            \"description\": s.get(\"description\", \"\")[:400],\n",
        "        })\n",
        "    return videos\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Get available qualities (no download) ────────────────────────────\n",
        "def get_available_qualities(video_id):\n",
        "    with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(YDL_BASE_OPTS) as ydl:\n",
        "        info = ydl.extract_info(\n",
        "            f\"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={video_id}\",\n",
        "            download=False\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        heights = set()\n",
        "        for f in info.get(\"formats\", []):\n",
        "            h = f.get(\"height\")\n",
        "            if h and f.get(\"vcodec\", \"none\") != \"none\":\n",
        "                heights.add(h)\n",
        "\n",
        "        order     = [2160, 1440, 1080, 720, 480, 360, 240, 144]\n",
        "        available = [f\"{h}p\" for h in order if h in heights]\n",
        "        print(f\"📋 Available qualities: {available}\")\n",
        "        return available, info.get(\"title\", \"\"), int(info.get(\"duration\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Download + merge video and audio via ffmpeg ───────────────────────\n",
        "def download_and_merge(video_id, quality_label):\n",
        "    height   = quality_label.replace(\"p\", \"\")\n",
        "    out_path = f\"/tmp/yt_{video_id}_{height}p.mp4\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Return cached file if already downloaded\n",
        "    if os.path.exists(out_path):\n",
        "        size_mb = os.path.getsize(out_path) / 1024 / 1024\n",
        "        print(f\"✅ Using cached {quality_label} ({size_mb:.1f} MB)\")\n",
        "        return out_path\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"⬇️  Downloading {quality_label} — merging video + audio with ffmpeg...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    ydl_opts = {\n",
        "        **YDL_BASE_OPTS,\n",
        "        \"quiet\":               False,\n",
        "        \"no_warnings\":         True,\n",
        "        \"format\":              (\n",
        "            f\"bestvideo[height<={height}][ext=mp4]+bestaudio[ext=m4a]\"\n",
        "            f\"/bestvideo[height<={height}]+bestaudio\"\n",
        "            f\"/best[height<={height}]\"\n",
        "        ),\n",
        "        \"outtmpl\":             f\"/tmp/yt_{video_id}_{height}p.%(ext)s\",\n",
        "        \"merge_output_format\": \"mp4\",\n",
        "        \"postprocessors\": [{\n",
        "            \"key\":            \"FFmpegVideoConvertor\",\n",
        "            \"preferedformat\": \"mp4\",\n",
        "        }],\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:\n",
        "        ydl.download([f\"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={video_id}\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Find and rename if yt-dlp added a different extension\n",
        "    for fname in os.listdir(\"/tmp\"):\n",
        "        if fname.startswith(f\"yt_{video_id}_{height}p\") and fname.endswith(\".mp4\"):\n",
        "            actual = f\"/tmp/{fname}\"\n",
        "            if actual != out_path:\n",
        "                os.rename(actual, out_path)\n",
        "            break\n",
        "\n",
        "    size_mb = os.path.getsize(out_path) / 1024 / 1024\n",
        "    print(f\"✅ Done! {quality_label} saved ({size_mb:.1f} MB)\")\n",
        "    return out_path\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Convert local mp4 to base64 data URL ─────────────────────────────\n",
        "def file_to_data_url(path):\n",
        "    with open(path, \"rb\") as f:\n",
        "        data = base64.b64encode(f.read()).decode()\n",
        "    return f\"data:video/mp4;base64,{data}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"✅ Cell 3 ready!\")"
      ],

        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"
        },
        "id": "lM4ptjBxkhtb",
        "outputId": "6acf6ad9-15b1-41c2-83ee-be8bddef5b6e"

      "execution_count": null,

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "def get_stream_urls(video_id):\n",
        "    quality_map = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "    ydl_opts = {\n",
        "        **YDL_BASE_OPTS,\n",
        "        \"quiet\": True,\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:\n",
        "        info = ydl.extract_info(\n",
        "            f\"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={video_id}\",\n",
        "            download=False\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        title    = info.get(\"title\", \"\")\n",
        "        duration = int(info.get(\"duration\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "        order = [1080, 720, 480, 360, 240, 144]\n",
        "        seen  = set()\n",
        "\n",
        "        for f in info.get(\"formats\", []):\n",
        "            h      = f.get(\"height\")\n",
        "            vcodec = f.get(\"vcodec\", \"none\")\n",
        "            acodec = f.get(\"acodec\", \"none\")\n",
        "            url    = f.get(\"url\", \"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            if (h and h in order\n",
        "                and vcodec != \"none\"\n",
        "                and acodec != \"none\"\n",
        "                and url\n",
        "                and h not in seen):\n",
        "\n",
        "                quality_map[f\"{h}p\"] = url\n",
        "                seen.add(h)\n",
        "\n",
        "        quality_map = dict(\n",
        "            sorted(quality_map.items(),\n",
        "                   key=lambda x: int(x[0].replace(\"p\", \"\")),\n",
        "                   reverse=True)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"✅ Found {len(quality_map)} combined streams: {list(quality_map.keys())}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return quality_map, title, duration"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "EPogLliTD49U"
      },
      "execution_count": 33,
      "outputs": []
    },

        "  .yt-meta    { color:#888; font-size:12px; }\n",
        "  .yt-player-box { grid-column:1/-1; background:#111; border-radius:10px; padding:16px; }\n",
        "  .yt-video   { width:100%; border-radius:8px; max-height:480px; background:#000; }\n",
        "  .yt-back    { background:none; border:1.5px solid #444; color:#f1f1f1;\n",
        "                padding:6px 18px; border-radius:20px; cursor:pointer;\n",
        "                font-size:13px; margin-bottom:14px; transition:background .15s; }\n",
        "  .yt-back:hover { background:#333; }\n",
        "  .yt-vt   { color:#f1f1f1; font-size:18px; font-weight:700; margin:12px 0 4px; }\n",
        "  .yt-vm   { color:#aaa; font-size:13px; margin-bottom:10px; }\n",
        "  .yt-vdesc{ color:#ccc; font-size:13px; line-height:1.7; background:#1a1a1a;\n",
        "             border-radius:8px; padding:12px 14px; white-space:pre-wrap; }\n",
        "  .yt-loading { color:#aaa; font-size:14px; padding:20px 0; }\n",
        "</style>\n",
        "\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def card_html(v):\n",
        "    t = v['title'].replace('\"','&quot;').replace('<','&lt;')\n",
        "    return f\"\"\"\n",
        "    <div class=\"yt-card\" id=\"card-{v['id']}\" onclick=\"loadVideo('{v['id']}')\">\n",
        "      <div class=\"yt-thumb-wrap\">\n",
        "        <img class=\"yt-thumb\" src=\"{v['thumbnail']}\" />\n",
        "        <span class=\"yt-badge\">{v['duration']}</span>\n",
        "      </div>\n",
        "      <div class=\"yt-info\">\n",
        "        <div class=\"yt-title\">{t}</div>\n",
        "        <div class=\"yt-channel\">{v['channel']}</div>\n",
        "        <div class=\"yt-meta\">{fmt_num(v['views'])} views · {v['published']}</div>\n",
        "      </div>\n",
        "    </div>\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def render_grid(videos):\n",
        "    cards = \"\".join(card_html(v) for v in videos)\n",
        "    return f\"\"\"{CSS}\n",
        "    <script>\n",
        "    function loadVideo(vid) {{\n",
        "        // Tell Python to fetch the stream URL\n",
        "        var kernel = IPython.notebook.kernel;\n",
        "        kernel.execute(\"_load_video('\" + vid + \"')\");\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "    function showPlayer(url, title, views, likes, date, desc) {{\n",
        "        // Hide all cards\n",
        "        document.querySelectorAll('.yt-card').forEach(c => c.style.display='none');\n",
        "        var box = document.getElementById('player-box');\n",
        "        box.style.display = 'block';\n",
        "        box.innerHTML = `\n",
        "          <button class=\"yt-back\" onclick=\"hidePlayer()\">← Back</button>\n",
        "          <video class=\"yt-video\" controls autoplay>\n",
        "            <source src=\"${{url}}\" type=\"video/mp4\">\n",
        "          </video>\n",
        "          <p class=\"yt-vt\">${{title}}</p>\n",
        "          <p class=\"yt-vm\">${{views}} views &nbsp;·&nbsp; 👍 ${{likes}} &nbsp;·&nbsp; ${{date}}</p>\n",
        "          <div class=\"yt-vdesc\">${{desc}}</div>\n",
        "        `;\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "    function hidePlayer() {{\n",
        "        document.getElementById('player-box').style.display = 'none';\n",
        "        document.querySelectorAll('.yt-card').forEach(c => c.style.display='block');\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "    </script>\n",
        "    <div class=\"yt-wrap\">\n",
        "      <div class=\"yt-grid\">\n",
        "        {cards}\n",
        "        <div id=\"player-box\" class=\"yt-player-box\" style=\"display:none;\"></div>\n",
        "      </div>\n",
        "    </div>\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"✅ Renderer ready!\")"
      ],

        "}\n",
        ".yt-vdesc-text.expanded { max-height: 600px; }\n",
        ".yt-vdesc-toggle { color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; margin-top: 6px; font-weight: 600; }\n",
        "\n",
        "/* Loading shimmer */\n",
        "@keyframes shimmer {\n",
        "  0%   { background-position: -200% 0; }\n",
        "  100% { background-position:  200% 0; }\n",
        "}\n",
        ".yt-shimmer {\n",
        "  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #1a1a1a 25%, #2a2a2a 50%, #1a1a1a 75%);\n",
        "  background-size: 200% 100%;\n",
        "  animation: shimmer 1.5s infinite;\n",
        "  border-radius: 8px;\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "/* Stagger card animation */\n",
        "@keyframes fadeUp {\n",
        "  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(20px); }\n",
        "  to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }\n",
        "}\n",
        ".yt-card { animation: fadeUp 0.4s ease both; }\n",
        "</style>\n",
        "\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# Avatar color palette\n",
        "AVATAR_COLORS = [\n",
        "    \"#e53935\",\"#d81b60\",\"#8e24aa\",\"#3949ab\",\n",
        "    \"#1e88e5\",\"#00897b\",\"#43a047\",\"#f4511e\",\n",
        "    \"#6d4c41\",\"#00acc1\",\"#7cb342\",\"#fb8c00\",\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "def avatar_color(name):\n",
        "    return AVATAR_COLORS[sum(ord(c) for c in name) % len(AVATAR_COLORS)]\n",
        "\n",
        "def card_html(v):\n",
        "    t  = v['title'].replace('<','&lt;').replace('\"','&quot;')\n",
        "    ch = v['channel'].replace('<','&lt;')\n",
        "    av = v['channel'][0].upper() if v['channel'] else \"?\"\n",
        "    col = avatar_color(v['channel'])\n",
        "    views = fmt_num(v['views'])\n",
        "    return f\"\"\"\n",
        "    <div class=\"yt-card\" id=\"card-{v['id']}\">\n",
        "      <div class=\"yt-thumb-wrap\">\n",
        "        <img class=\"yt-thumb\" src=\"{v['thumbnail']}\"\n",
        "             onerror=\"this.style.background='#222'\" loading=\"lazy\" />\n",
        "        <div class=\"yt-play-overlay\">\n",
        "          <div class=\"yt-play-icon\">\n",
        "            <svg width=\"22\" height=\"22\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"white\">\n",
        "              <path d=\"M8 5v14l11-7z\"/>\n",
        "            </svg>\n",
        "          </div>\n",
        "        </div>\n",
        "        <span class=\"yt-badge\">{v['duration']}</span>\n",
        "      </div>\n",
        "      <div class=\"yt-info\">\n",
        "        <div class=\"yt-avatar\" style=\"background:{col}\">{av}</div>\n",
        "        <div class=\"yt-info-text\">\n",
        "          <div class=\"yt-title\">{t}</div>\n",
        "          <div class=\"yt-channel\">{ch}</div>\n",
        "          <div class=\"yt-meta\">{views} views · {v['published']}</div>\n",
        "        </div>\n",
        "      </div>\n",
        "    </div>\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def player_html(quality_map, v):\n",
        "    t   = v['title'].replace('<','&lt;').replace('`','').replace('\"','&quot;')\n",
        "    desc = v['description'].replace('<','&lt;').replace('`','')[:800]\n",
        "    views = fmt_num(v['views'])\n",
        "    likes = fmt_num(v['likes'])\n",
        "    ch    = v['channel'].replace('<','&lt;')\n",
        "    av    = v['channel'][0].upper() if v['channel'] else \"?\"\n",
        "    col   = avatar_color(v['channel'])\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Build quality options\n",
        "    quality_options = \"\"\n",
        "    first_url = \"\"\n",
        "    quality_js_map = \"{\"\n",
        "    for i, (label, url) in enumerate(quality_map.items()):\n",
        "        sel = \"selected\" if i == 0 else \"\"\n",
        "        if i == 0:\n",
        "            first_url = url\n",
        "        quality_options += f'<option value=\"{label}\" {sel}>{label}</option>'\n",
        "        quality_js_map  += f'\"{label}\": `{url}`,'\n",
        "    quality_js_map += \"}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    pid = f\"player-{v['id']}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    return f\"\"\"\n",
        "    {CSS}\n",
        "    <div class=\"yt-player-root\" id=\"{pid}\">\n",
        "\n",
        "      <!-- Video wrap -->\n",
        "      <div class=\"yt-video-wrap\" id=\"wrap-{v['id']}\" onclick=\"togglePlay('{v['id']}')\">\n",
        "        <video id=\"vid-{v['id']}\" preload=\"metadata\"\n",
        "               onplay=\"onPlayState('{v['id']}',false)\"\n",
        "               onpause=\"onPlayState('{v['id']}',true)\"\n",
        "               ontimeupdate=\"onTimeUpdate('{v['id']}')\"\n",
        "               onprogress=\"onBuffer('{v['id']}')\"\n",
        "               onloadedmetadata=\"onMeta('{v['id']}')\"\n",
        "               onvolumechange=\"onVolChange('{v['id']}')\">\n",
        "          <source src=\"{first_url}\" type=\"video/mp4\">\n",
        "        </video>\n",
        "\n",
        "        <!-- Controls -->\n",
        "        <div class=\"yt-controls\" id=\"ctrl-{v['id']}\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation()\">\n",
        "\n",
        "          <!-- Progress -->\n",
        "          <div class=\"yt-progress-wrap\" id=\"prog-{v['id']}\"\n",
        "               onmousedown=\"seekStart(event,'{v['id']}')\"\n",
        "               onmousemove=\"seekHover(event,'{v['id']}')\">\n",
        "            <div class=\"yt-progress-buf\"  id=\"buf-{v['id']}\"  style=\"width:0%\"></div>\n",
        "            <div class=\"yt-progress-fill\" id=\"fill-{v['id']}\" style=\"width:0%\"></div>\n",
        "            <div class=\"yt-progress-thumb\" id=\"thumb-{v['id']}\" style=\"left:0%\"></div>\n",
        "          </div>\n",
        "\n",
        "          <!-- Controls row -->\n",
        "          <div class=\"yt-ctrl-row\">\n",
        "\n",
        "            <!-- Play/Pause -->\n",
        "            <button class=\"yt-ctrl-btn\" id=\"playbtn-{v['id']}\"\n",
        "                    onclick=\"togglePlay('{v['id']}')\">\n",
        "              <svg viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" id=\"playicon-{v['id']}\">\n",
        "                <path d=\"M8 5v14l11-7z\"/>\n",
        "              </svg>\n",
        "            </button>\n",
        "\n",
        "            <!-- Volume -->\n",
        "            <div class=\"yt-vol-wrap\">\n",
        "              <button class=\"yt-ctrl-btn\" onclick=\"toggleMute('{v['id']}')\">\n",
        "                <svg viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" id=\"volicon-{v['id']}\">\n",
        "                  <path d=\"M3 9v6h4l5 5V4L7 9H3zm13.5 3c0-1.77-1.02-3.29-2.5-4.03v8.05c1.48-.73 2.5-2.25 2.5-4.02z\"/>\n",
        "                </svg>\n",
        "              </button>\n",
        "              <input class=\"yt-vol-slider\" id=\"vol-{v['id']}\" type=\"range\"\n",
        "                     min=\"0\" max=\"1\" step=\"0.05\" value=\"1\"\n",
        "                     oninput=\"setVol('{v['id']}',this.value)\"\n",
        "                     onclick=\"event.stopPropagation()\">\n",
        "            </div>\n",
        "\n",
        "            <!-- Time -->\n",
        "            <span class=\"yt-time\" id=\"time-{v['id']}\">0:00 / 0:00</span>\n",
        "\n",
        "            <!-- Right side -->\n",
        "            <div class=\"yt-ctrl-right\">\n",
        "\n",
        "              <!-- Speed -->\n",
        "              <select class=\"yt-select\" onchange=\"setSpeed('{v['id']}',this.value)\"\n",
        "                      onclick=\"event.stopPropagation()\">\n",
        "                <option value=\"0.25\">0.25×</option>\n",
        "                <option value=\"0.5\">0.5×</option>\n",
        "                <option value=\"0.75\">0.75×</option>\n",
        "                <option value=\"1\" selected>1×</option>\n",
        "                <option value=\"1.25\">1.25×</option>\n",

        "              </svg>\n",

        "              </svg>\n",

        "          </div>\n",

        "        <div style=\"display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-bottom:14px;\">\n",
        "          <div class=\"yt-avatar\" style=\"background:{col};width:40px;height:40px;font-size:16px;\">{av}</div>\n",
        "          <div>\n",
        "            <div style=\"font-weight:600;font-size:14px;\">{ch}</div>\n",
        "          </div>\n",
        "        </div>\n",
        "\n",

        "        <div class=\"yt-vdesc-wrap\" onclick=\"toggleDesc('{v['id']}')\">\n",

        "          <div class=\"yt-vdesc-text\" id=\"desc-{v['id']}\">{desc}</div>\n",

        "        </div>\n",
        "      </div>\n",
        "\n",
        "    </div><!-- /player-root -->\n",
        "\n",
        "    <script>\n",
        "    // Quality map for this video\n",
        "    var _qmap_{v['id'].replace('-','_')} = {quality_js_map};\n",
        "\n",
        "    function getVid(id)  {{ return document.getElementById('vid-'  + id); }}\n",
        "    function getWrap(id) {{ return document.getElementById('wrap-' + id); }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function togglePlay(id) {{\n",
        "      var v = getVid(id);\n",
        "      if (!v) return;\n",
        "      v.paused ? v.play() : v.pause();\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function onPlayState(id, isPaused) {{\n",
        "      var icon = document.getElementById('playicon-' + id);\n",
        "      var wrap = getWrap(id);\n",
        "      if (!icon) return;\n",
        "      if (isPaused) {{\n",
        "        icon.innerHTML = '<path d=\"M8 5v14l11-7z\"/>';\n",
        "        wrap.classList.add('paused');\n",
        "      }} else {{\n",
        "        icon.innerHTML = '<path d=\"M6 19h4V5H6v14zm8-14v14h4V5h-4z\"/>';\n",
        "        wrap.classList.remove('paused');\n",
        "      }}\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function onTimeUpdate(id) {{\n",
        "      var v    = getVid(id);\n",
        "      var fill = document.getElementById('fill-' + id);\n",
        "      var thmb = document.getElementById('thumb-' + id);\n",
        "      var tdisp= document.getElementById('time-'  + id);\n",
        "      if (!v || !fill) return;\n",
        "      var pct = v.duration ? (v.currentTime / v.duration * 100) : 0;\n",
        "      fill.style.width = pct + '%';\n",
        "      thmb.style.left  = pct + '%';\n",
        "      tdisp.textContent = fmtTime(v.currentTime) + ' / ' + fmtTime(v.duration || 0);\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function onBuffer(id) {{\n",
        "      var v   = getVid(id);\n",
        "      var buf = document.getElementById('buf-' + id);\n",
        "      if (!v || !buf || !v.buffered.length) return;\n",
        "      var pct = (v.buffered.end(v.buffered.length-1) / v.duration * 100);\n",
        "      buf.style.width = pct + '%';\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function onMeta(id) {{\n",
        "      var v    = getVid(id);\n",
        "      var tdisp= document.getElementById('time-' + id);\n",
        "      if (v && tdisp) tdisp.textContent = '0:00 / ' + fmtTime(v.duration);\n",
        "      v && v.play();\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function seekStart(e, id) {{\n",
        "      var v    = getVid(id);\n",
        "      var prog = document.getElementById('prog-' + id);\n",
        "      if (!v || !prog) return;\n",
        "      var rect = prog.getBoundingClientRect();\n",
        "      var pct  = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width));\n",
        "      v.currentTime = pct * v.duration;\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function seekHover(e, id) {{\n",
        "      if (e.buttons !== 1) return;\n",
        "      seekStart(e, id);\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function setVol(id, val) {{\n",
        "      var v = getVid(id);\n",
        "      if (v) {{ v.volume = val; v.muted = (val == 0); }}\n",
        "      updateVolIcon(id, val, val == 0);\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function onVolChange(id) {{\n",
        "      var v = getVid(id);\n",
        "      if (!v) return;\n",
        "      updateVolIcon(id, v.volume, v.muted);\n",
        "      var slider = document.getElementById('vol-' + id);\n",
        "      if (slider) slider.value = v.muted ? 0 : v.volume;\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function updateVolIcon(id, vol, muted) {{\n",
        "      var icon = document.getElementById('volicon-' + id);\n",
        "      if (!icon) return;\n",
        "      if (muted || vol == 0)\n",
        "        icon.innerHTML = '<path d=\"M16.5 12c0-1.77-1.02-3.29-2.5-4.03v2.21l2.45 2.45c.03-.2.05-.41.05-.63zm2.5 0c0 .94-.2 1.82-.54 2.64l1.51 1.51C20.63 14.91 21 13.5 21 12c0-4.28-2.99-7.86-7-8.77v2.06c2.89.86 5 3.54 5 6.71zM4.27 3L3 4.27 7.73 9H3v6h4l5 5v-6.73l4.25 4.25c-.67.52-1.42.93-2.25 1.18v2.06c1.38-.31 2.63-.95 3.69-1.81L19.73 21 21 19.73l-9-9L4.27 3zM12 4L9.91 6.09 12 8.18V4z\"/>';\n",
        "      else if (vol < 0.5)\n",
        "        icon.innerHTML = '<path d=\"M18.5 12c0-1.77-1.02-3.29-2.5-4.03v8.05c1.48-.73 2.5-2.25 2.5-4.02zM5 9v6h4l5 5V4L9 9H5z\"/>';\n",
        "      else\n",
        "        icon.innerHTML = '<path d=\"M3 9v6h4l5 5V4L7 9H3zm13.5 3c0-1.77-1.02-3.29-2.5-4.03v8.05c1.48-.73 2.5-2.25 2.5-4.02zM14 3.23v2.06c2.89.86 5 3.54 5 6.71s-2.11 5.85-5 6.71v2.06c4.01-.91 7-4.49 7-8.77s-2.99-7.86-7-8.77z\"/>';\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function toggleMute(id) {{\n",
        "      var v = getVid(id);\n",
        "      if (!v) return;\n",
        "      v.muted = !v.muted;\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function setSpeed(id, val) {{\n",
        "      var v = getVid(id);\n",
        "      if (v) v.playbackRate = parseFloat(val);\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function setQuality(id, label) {{\n",
        "      var v    = getVid(id);\n",
        "      var qmap = window['_qmap_' + id.replace(/-/g,'_')];\n",
        "      if (!v || !qmap || !qmap[label]) return;\n",
        "      var t = v.currentTime, paused = v.paused;\n",
        "      v.src = qmap[label];\n",
        "      v.load();\n",
        "      v.currentTime = t;\n",
        "      if (!paused) v.play();\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function goFullscreen(id) {{\n",
        "      var wrap = getWrap(id);\n",
        "      if (!wrap) return;\n",
        "      if (wrap.requestFullscreen)             wrap.requestFullscreen();\n",
        "      else if (wrap.webkitRequestFullscreen)  wrap.webkitRequestFullscreen();\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function toggleLike(id) {{\n",
        "      var btn = document.getElementById('likebtn-' + id);\n",
        "      if (btn) btn.classList.toggle('liked');\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function toggleDesc(id) {{\n",
        "      var d  = document.getElementById('desc-' + id);\n",
        "      var tg = document.getElementById('desc-toggle-' + id);\n",
        "      if (!d) return;\n",
        "      var exp = d.classList.toggle('expanded');\n",
        "      tg.textContent = exp ? '▲ Show less' : '▼ Show more';\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    function fmtTime(s) {{\n",
        "      if (!s || isNaN(s)) return '0:00';\n",
        "      var h = Math.floor(s/3600), m = Math.floor((s%3600)/60), sec = Math.floor(s%60);\n",
        "      if (h) return h+':'+pad(m)+':'+pad(sec);\n",
        "      return m+':'+pad(sec);\n",
        "    }}\n",
        "    function pad(n) {{ return n < 10 ? '0'+n : ''+n; }}\n",
        "\n",
        "    // Store quality map globally for JS access\n",
        "    window['_qmap_{v['id'].replace('-','_')}'] = {quality_js_map};\n",
        "    </script>\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"✅ Player + Card renderer ready!\")"

        "from IPython import get_ipython\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- Global video store ---\n",
        "_video_store = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "def _load_video(video_id):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Called from JS — fetches stream URL via yt-dlp and injects into page.\"\"\"\n",
        "    status_lbl.value = \"⏳ Fetching stream URL...\"\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        stream_url, title, dur = get_stream_url(video_id)\n",
        "        v = _video_store.get(video_id, {})\n",
        "        js = f\"\"\"\n",
        "        showPlayer(\n",
        "          \"{stream_url}\",\n",
        "          `{v.get('title','').replace('`','').replace('\"','')}`,\n",
        "          \"{fmt_num(v.get('views',0))}\",\n",
        "          \"{fmt_num(v.get('likes',0))}\",\n",
        "          \"{v.get('published','')}\",\n",
        "          `{v.get('description','').replace('`','').replace('\"','')[:300]}`\n",
        "        );\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        with output_area:\n",
        "            display(HTML(f\"<script>{js}</script>\"))\n",
        "        status_lbl.value = \"▶️ Playing!\"\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        status_lbl.value = f\"❌ Stream error: {e}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# Register as IPython-accessible global\n",
        "get_ipython().user_ns[\"_load_video\"] = _load_video\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- Widgets ---\n",
        "search_box  = widgets.Text(placeholder=\"🔍  Search videos...\",\n",
        "                           layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"60%\", height=\"36px\"))\n",
        "search_btn  = widgets.Button(description=\"Search\", button_style=\"danger\",\n",
        "                             layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"100px\", height=\"36px\"))\n",
        "trend_btn   = widgets.Button(description=\"🔥 Trending\",\n",
        "                             layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"120px\", height=\"36px\"))\n",
        "status_lbl  = widgets.Label(value=\"\")\n",
        "output_area = widgets.Output()\n",
        "\n",
        "top_bar = widgets.HBox([search_box, search_btn, trend_btn, status_lbl],\n",
        "                       layout=widgets.Layout(margin=\"0 0 12px 0\",\n",
        "                                            align_items=\"center\", gap=\"8px\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "def show_videos(videos, heading=\"\"):\n",
        "    global _video_store\n",
        "    _video_store = {v[\"id\"]: v for v in videos}\n",
        "    with output_area:\n",
        "        clear_output(wait=True)\n",
        "        h = f\"<p style='color:#aaa;font-size:13px;margin:0 0 8px'>{heading}</p>\" if heading else \"\"\n",
        "        display(HTML(h + render_grid(videos)))\n",
        "\n",
        "def on_search(b):\n",

        "#CELL 5 V-1 L\n"

        "id": "J67t2KEegvBC"

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import ipywidgets as widgets\n",
        "from IPython.display import display, HTML, clear_output\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Shared state ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "_video_store = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Layout areas ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "status_lbl  = widgets.Label(value=\"\")\n",
        "player_out  = widgets.Output()   # shows the video player\n",
        "grid_out    = widgets.Output()   # shows the card grid\n",
        "search_box  = widgets.Text(\n",
        "    placeholder=\"🔍  Search videos...\",\n",
        "    layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"60%\", height=\"36px\")\n",
        ")\n",
        "search_btn  = widgets.Button(\n",
        "    description=\"Search\", button_style=\"danger\",\n",
        "    layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"100px\", height=\"36px\")\n",
        ")\n",
        "trend_btn   = widgets.Button(\n",
        "    description=\"🔥 Trending\",\n",
        "    layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"120px\", height=\"36px\")\n",
        ")\n",
        "back_btn    = widgets.Button(\n",
        "    description=\"← Back to results\",\n",
        "    layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"160px\", height=\"36px\")\n",
        ")\n",
        "back_btn.style.button_color = \"#1a1a1a\"\n",
        "\n",
        "top_bar = widgets.HBox(\n",
        "    [search_box, search_btn, trend_btn, status_lbl],\n",
        "    layout=widgets.Layout(margin=\"0 0 12px 0\", align_items=\"center\", gap=\"8px\")\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Video click handler ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def on_video_click(video_id):\n",
        "    v = _video_store.get(video_id)\n",
        "    if not v:\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    status_lbl.value = f\"⏳ Loading '{v['title'][:30]}...' (yt-dlp fetching stream)\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Show player area, hide grid\n",
        "    with player_out:\n",
        "        clear_output(wait=True)\n",
        "        display(back_btn)\n",
        "        display(HTML(CSS + \"<p style='color:#aaa'>⏳ Fetching direct stream URL, please wait 5-10 sec...</p>\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "    grid_out.clear_output()\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        stream_url, _, _ = get_stream_url(video_id)\n",
        "        with player_out:\n",
        "            clear_output(wait=True)\n",
        "            display(back_btn)\n",
        "            display(HTML(CSS + player_html(stream_url, v)))\n",
        "        status_lbl.value = \"▶️ Playing!\"\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        with player_out:\n",
        "            clear_output(wait=True)\n",
        "            display(back_btn)\n",
        "            display(HTML(f\"<p style='color:red'>❌ Could not load video: {e}</p>\"))\n",
        "        status_lbl.value = f\"❌ Error: {e}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Build grid of widget buttons ──────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def make_grid(videos):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Each video card is an HTML widget + invisible button overlay.\"\"\"\n",
        "    global _video_store\n",
        "    _video_store = {v[\"id\"]: v for v in videos}\n",
        "\n",
        "    rows = []\n",
        "    row  = []\n",
        "    for i, v in enumerate(videos):\n",
        "        # Create a button that looks like a card using HTML widget\n",
        "        card = widgets.HTML(value=card_html(v))\n",
        "        btn  = widgets.Button(\n",
        "            description=\"▶ Play\",\n",
        "            layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"100%\", height=\"32px\", margin=\"2px 0 0 0\")\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        btn.style.button_color = \"#ff0000\"\n",
        "        btn.style.text_color   = \"#ffffff\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        vid_id = v[\"id\"]\n",
        "        btn.on_click(lambda b, vid=vid_id: on_video_click(vid))\n",
        "\n",
        "        cell = widgets.VBox(\n",
        "            [card, btn],\n",
        "            layout=widgets.Layout(\n",
        "                border=\"1px solid #2a2a2a\",\n",
        "                border_radius=\"10px\",\n",
        "                overflow=\"hidden\",\n",
        "                background_color=\"#1a1a1a\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        row.append(cell)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # 3 cards per row\n",
        "        if len(row) == 3 or i == len(videos) - 1:\n",
        "            rows.append(widgets.HBox(\n",
        "                row,\n",
        "                layout=widgets.Layout(gap=\"12px\", margin=\"0 0 12px 0\")\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "            row = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    return widgets.VBox(rows)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Back button ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def on_back(b):\n",
        "    with player_out:\n",
        "        clear_output()\n",
        "    # Re-render last grid\n",
        "    with grid_out:\n",
        "        clear_output(wait=True)\n",
        "        display(widgets.HTML(CSS))\n",
        "        display(_last_grid)\n",
        "    status_lbl.value = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "back_btn.on_click(on_back)\n",
        "_last_grid = None\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Show videos ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def show_videos(videos, heading=\"\"):\n",
        "    global _last_grid\n",
        "    with player_out:\n",
        "        clear_output()\n",
        "    grid = make_grid(videos)\n",
        "    _last_grid = grid\n",
        "    with grid_out:\n",
        "        clear_output(wait=True)\n",
        "        if heading:\n",
        "            display(HTML(f\"<p style='color:#aaa;font-size:13px;margin:0 0 8px'>{heading}</p>\"))\n",
        "        display(HTML(CSS))\n",
        "        display(grid)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Search / Trending ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def on_search(b):\n",
        "    q = search_box.value.strip()\n",
        "    if not q: return\n",
        "    status_lbl.value = \"⏳ Searching...\"\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        videos = search_videos(q)\n",
        "        show_videos(videos, f'Results for \"{q}\" — {len(videos)} videos')\n",
        "        status_lbl.value = f\"✅ {len(videos)} results\"\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        status_lbl.value = f\"❌ {e}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def on_trending(b):\n",
        "    status_lbl.value = \"⏳ Loading trending...\"\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        videos = get_trending()\n",
        "        show_videos(videos, \"🔥 Trending in India\")\n",
        "        status_lbl.value = f\"✅ {len(videos)} trending\"\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        status_lbl.value = f\"❌ {e}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "search_btn.on_click(on_search)\n",
        "search_box.on_submit(on_search)\n",
        "trend_btn.on_click(on_trending)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Display everything ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "display(HTML(CSS))\n",
        "display(top_bar, player_out, grid_out)\n",
        "on_trending(None)"

        "import ipywidgets as widgets\n",
        "from IPython.display import display, HTML, clear_output\n",
        "\n",
        "_video_store = {}\n",
        "_last_grid   = None\n",
        "\n",
        "status_lbl = widgets.Label(value=\"\")\n",
        "player_out = widgets.Output()\n",
        "grid_out   = widgets.Output()\n",
        "\n",
        "search_box = widgets.Text(\n",
        "    placeholder=\"🔍  Search anything...\",\n",
        "    layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"58%\", height=\"38px\")\n",
        ")\n",
        "search_btn = widgets.Button(\n",
        "    description=\"Search\",\n",
        "    button_style=\"danger\",\n",
        "    layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"100px\", height=\"38px\")\n",
        ")\n",
        "trend_btn = widgets.Button(\n",
        "    description=\"🔥 Trending\",\n",
        "    layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"120px\", height=\"38px\")\n",
        ")\n",
        "back_btn = widgets.Button(\n",
        "    description=\"← Back\",\n",
        "    layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"90px\", height=\"34px\")\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "top_bar = widgets.HBox(\n",
        "    [search_box, search_btn, trend_btn, status_lbl],\n",
        "    layout=widgets.Layout(\n",
        "        margin=\"12px 0 4px 0\",\n",
        "        align_items=\"center\",\n",
        "        gap=\"8px\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Video click ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def on_video_click(video_id):\n",
        "    v = _video_store.get(video_id, {})\n",
        "    title_short = v.get('title','')[:35]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Show shimmer loading state immediately\n",
        "    with player_out:\n",
        "        clear_output(wait=True)\n",
        "        display(back_btn)\n",
        "        display(HTML(f\"\"\"\n",
        "        {CSS}\n",
        "        <div style=\"background:#111;border-radius:16px;overflow:hidden;border:1px solid #2a2a2a;\">\n",
        "          <div class=\"yt-shimmer\" style=\"width:100%;aspect-ratio:16/9;\"></div>\n",
        "          <div style=\"padding:18px;\">\n",
        "            <div class=\"yt-shimmer\" style=\"height:22px;width:70%;margin-bottom:12px;\"></div>\n",
        "            <div class=\"yt-shimmer\" style=\"height:14px;width:40%;\"></div>\n",
        "          </div>\n",
        "        </div>\n",
        "        <p style=\"color:#888;font-size:13px;margin-top:10px;\">\n",
        "          ⏳ Fetching stream URLs for all qualities... (5-15 sec)\n",
        "        </p>\n",
        "        \"\"\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "    with grid_out:\n",
        "        clear_output()\n",
        "\n",
        "    status_lbl.value = f\"⏳ Loading '{title_short}...'\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        quality_map, _, _ = get_stream_urls(video_id)\n",
        "        with player_out:\n",
        "            clear_output(wait=True)\n",
        "            display(back_btn)\n",
        "            display(HTML(player_html(quality_map, v)))\n",
        "        status_lbl.value = f\"▶ {len(quality_map)} qualities available\"\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        with player_out:\n",
        "            clear_output(wait=True)\n",
        "            display(back_btn)\n",
        "            display(HTML(f\"<p style='color:#ff4444;padding:20px'>❌ {e}</p>\"))\n",
        "        status_lbl.value = \"❌ Error loading video\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Grid builder ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def make_grid(videos):\n",
        "    global _video_store\n",
        "    _video_store = {v[\"id\"]: v for v in videos}\n",
        "\n",
        "    rows, row = [], []\n",
        "    for i, v in enumerate(videos):\n",
        "        card = widgets.HTML(value=card_html(v))\n",
        "        btn  = widgets.Button(\n",
        "            description=\"▶  Play\",\n",
        "            layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"100%\", height=\"34px\", margin=\"0\")\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        btn.style.button_color = \"#ff2d2d\"\n",
        "        btn.style.text_color   = \"#ffffff\"\n",
        "        vid_id = v[\"id\"]\n",
        "        btn.on_click(lambda b, vid=vid_id: on_video_click(vid))\n",
        "\n",
        "        cell = widgets.VBox(\n",
        "            [card, btn],\n",
        "            layout=widgets.Layout(\n",
        "                overflow=\"hidden\",\n",
        "                border=\"1px solid #1e1e1e\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        row.append(cell)\n",
        "        if len(row) == 3 or i == len(videos) - 1:\n",
        "            rows.append(widgets.HBox(\n",
        "                row,\n",
        "                layout=widgets.Layout(gap=\"16px\", margin=\"0 0 16px 0\")\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "            row = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    return widgets.VBox(rows)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Back ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def on_back(b):\n",
        "    with player_out:\n",
        "        clear_output()\n",
        "    with grid_out:\n",
        "        clear_output(wait=True)\n",
        "        if _last_grid:\n",
        "            display(_last_grid[0])\n",
        "            display(_last_grid[1])\n",
        "    status_lbl.value = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "back_btn.on_click(on_back)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Show videos ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def show_videos(videos, heading=\"\"):\n",
        "    global _last_grid\n",
        "    with player_out:\n",
        "        clear_output()\n",
        "    grid = make_grid(videos)\n",
        "    heading_w = widgets.HTML(\n",
        "        value=f\"{CSS}<div class='yt-root'><div class='yt-heading'>{heading}</div></div>\"\n",
        "    ) if heading else widgets.HTML(value=CSS)\n",
        "    _last_grid = (heading_w, grid)\n",
        "    with grid_out:\n",
        "        clear_output(wait=True)\n",
        "        display(heading_w)\n",
        "        display(grid)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Handlers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def on_search(b):\n",
        "    q = search_box.value.strip()\n",
        "    if not q: return\n",
        "    status_lbl.value = \"⏳ Searching...\"\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        videos = search_videos(q)\n",
        "        show_videos(videos, f'Results for \"<span>{q}</span>\" — {len(videos)} videos')\n",
        "        status_lbl.value = f\"✅ {len(videos)} results\"\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        status_lbl.value = f\"❌ {e}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def on_trending(b):\n",
        "    status_lbl.value = \"⏳ Loading trending...\"\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        videos = get_trending()\n",
        "        show_videos(videos, \"🔥 <span>Trending</span> in India\")\n",
        "        status_lbl.value = f\"✅ {len(videos)} videos\"\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        status_lbl.value = f\"❌ {e}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "search_btn.on_click(on_search)\n",
        "search_box.on_submit(on_search)\n",
        "trend_btn.on_click(on_trending)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Launch ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "display(HTML(CSS))\n",
        "display(top_bar, player_out, grid_out)\n",
        "on_trending(None)"
      ],
